<p><b>Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</b><sup id="cite_ref-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-5">&#91;5&#93;</a></sup> is a <a href="/wiki/British_Overseas_Territories" title="British Overseas Territories">British Overseas Territory</a> located in the <a href="/wiki/Atlantic_Ocean" title="Atlantic Ocean">South Atlantic</a> and consisting of the island of <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena" title="Saint Helena">Saint Helena</a>, <a href="/wiki/Ascension_Island" title="Ascension Island">Ascension Island</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Archipelago" title="Archipelago">archipelago</a> of <a href="/wiki/Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Tristan da Cunha">Tristan da Cunha</a> (including <a href="/wiki/Gough_Island" title="Gough Island">Gough Island</a>). Its name was Saint Helena and Dependencies until 1 September 2009, when a new constitution came into force giving the three islands equal status as three territories, with a grouping under <a href="/wiki/The_Crown" title="The Crown">the Crown</a>.
</p>
<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" />
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="History">History</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: History">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1033289096">.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+link+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}</style><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/History_of_Saint_Helena" title="History of Saint Helena">History of Saint Helena</a></div>
<p>Of <a href="/wiki/Plate_tectonics" title="Plate tectonics">volcanic origin</a>, the islands of <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena" title="Saint Helena">Saint Helena</a>, Ascension Island, and <a href="/wiki/Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Tristan da Cunha">Tristan da Cunha</a> were all formerly separate colonies of the <a href="/wiki/Kingdom_of_England" title="Kingdom of England">English crown</a>, though separately discovered by several Portuguese explorers between 1502 and 1504.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Portuguese_discovery">Portuguese discovery</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Portuguese discovery">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Portugal" title="Portugal">Portuguese</a> found Saint Helena uninhabited, with an abundance of trees and <a href="/wiki/Fresh_water" title="Fresh water">fresh water</a>. They imported livestock, fruit trees and vegetables, and built a chapel and one or two houses. Though they formed no permanent settlement, the island became crucially important for the collection of food and as a rendezvous point for homebound voyages from Asia. English privateer <a href="/wiki/Francis_Drake" title="Francis Drake">Francis Drake</a> very probably located the island on the final lap of his circumnavigation of the world (1577–1580).<sup id="cite_ref-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-6">&#91;6&#93;</a></sup> Further visits by other English explorers followed, and, once St&#160;Helena's location was more widely known, English warships began to lie in wait in the area to attack Portuguese <a href="/wiki/Carracks" class="mw-redirect" title="Carracks">carracks</a> on their way home from <a href="/wiki/India" title="India">India</a>. In developing their Far East trade, the <a href="/wiki/Dutch_Republic" title="Dutch Republic">Dutch</a> also began to frequent the island and made a formal claim to it in 1633, but did not settle the isle and by 1651 largely abandoned it in favour of their colony at the <a href="/wiki/Cape_of_Good_Hope" title="Cape of Good Hope">Cape of Good Hope</a>.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="English_colonisation">English colonisation</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: English colonisation">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<figure class="mw-halign-left" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Thornton,_St_Helena.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1a/Thornton%2C_St_Helena.jpg/250px-Thornton%2C_St_Helena.jpg" decoding="async" width="250" height="167" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1a/Thornton%2C_St_Helena.jpg/375px-Thornton%2C_St_Helena.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/1a/Thornton%2C_St_Helena.jpg/500px-Thornton%2C_St_Helena.jpg 2x" data-file-width="782" data-file-height="522" /></a><figcaption><i>A View of the Town and Island of St&#160;Helena in the Atlantic Ocean belonging to the <a href="/wiki/English_East_India_Company" class="mw-redirect" title="English East India Company">English East India Company</a></i> (engraving <a href="https://en.wiktionary.org/wiki/circa" class="extiw" title="wiktionary:circa">c.</a> 1790)</figcaption></figure>
<p>In 1657, the English <a href="/wiki/East_India_Company" title="East India Company">East India Company</a> was granted a charter to govern Saint Helena by <a href="/wiki/Oliver_Cromwell" title="Oliver Cromwell">Oliver Cromwell</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-7">&#91;7&#93;</a></sup> and the following year the Company decided to fortify the island and colonise it with planters. The first governor, Captain John Dutton, arrived in 1659, and it is from this date that St Helena claims to be Britain's second oldest remaining colony, after <a href="/wiki/Bermuda" title="Bermuda">Bermuda</a>. A <a href="/wiki/High_Knoll_Fort" title="High Knoll Fort">fort</a> was completed and a number of houses were built. After <a href="/wiki/Stuart_Restoration" title="Stuart Restoration">the Restoration</a> of the British monarchy in 1660, the East India Company received a Royal Charter giving it the sole right to fortify and colonise the island. The fort was renamed <i>James Fort</i> and the town <i>Jamestown</i>, in honour of the Duke of York and heir apparent, later King <a href="/wiki/James_II_of_England_and_VII_of_Scotland" class="mw-redirect" title="James II of England and VII of Scotland">James II of England and VII of Scotland</a>.
</p>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands-113070.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8c/Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands-113070.jpg/220px-Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands-113070.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="147" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8c/Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands-113070.jpg/330px-Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands-113070.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/8c/Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands-113070.jpg/440px-Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands-113070.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2592" data-file-height="1728" /></a><figcaption><a href="/wiki/Gough_and_Inaccessible_Islands" class="mw-redirect" title="Gough and Inaccessible Islands">Gough and Inaccessible Islands</a> were declared <a href="/wiki/World_Heritage_Site" title="World Heritage Site">World Heritage Site</a> by <a href="/wiki/UNESCO" title="UNESCO">UNESCO</a> in 1995.</figcaption></figure>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Kingdom_of_England" title="Kingdom of England">Kingdom of England</a> became part of the new <a href="/wiki/Kingdom_of_Great_Britain" title="Kingdom of Great Britain">Kingdom of Great Britain</a> in 1707 and then the <a href="/wiki/United_Kingdom" title="United Kingdom">United Kingdom</a> in 1801; the <a href="/wiki/British_Empire" title="British Empire">British Empire</a> grew into a global <a href="/wiki/Great_power" title="Great power">great power</a>.  The island of Saint Helena became internationally known as the British government's chosen place of exile of <a href="/wiki/Napoleon_Bonaparte" class="mw-redirect" title="Napoleon Bonaparte">Napoleon Bonaparte</a>, who was detained on the island from October 1815 until his death on 5 May 1821, and it was made a British <a href="/wiki/Crown_colony" title="Crown colony">crown colony</a> in 1834 by the <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena_Act_1833" class="mw-redirect" title="Saint Helena Act 1833">Government of India Act 1833</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-crown_colony_1-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-crown_colony-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> Unoccupied <a href="/wiki/Ascension_Island" title="Ascension Island">Ascension Island</a> was garrisoned by the <a href="/wiki/Royal_Navy" title="Royal Navy">Royal Navy</a> on 22 October 1815, shortly after which the end of the Age of Sail made its difficult location in the equatorial <a href="/wiki/Doldrums" class="mw-redirect" title="Doldrums">doldrums</a> less important relative to its strategic importance as a centrally positioned naval coaling station. For similar reasons <a href="/wiki/Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Tristan da Cunha">Tristan da Cunha</a> was annexed as a dependency of the <a href="/wiki/Cape_Colony" title="Cape Colony">Cape Colony</a> (British South Africa) on 14 August 1816, at the settlement of the <a href="/wiki/Napoleonic_Wars" title="Napoleonic Wars">Napoleonic Wars</a>. For a short period just previously, Tristan da Cunha had been inhabited by a private American expedition who named the territory the <a href="/wiki/Islands_of_Refreshment" title="Islands of Refreshment">Islands of Refreshment</a>.
</p><p>The political union between these colonies began to take shape on 12 September 1922, when by <a href="/wiki/Letters_patent" title="Letters patent">letters patent</a> Ascension Island became a dependency of Saint Helena. Lightly populated Tristan da Cunha, even today little more than an outpost with a population of less than three hundred, followed suit on 12 January 1938. The three island groups shared this constitutional relationship until 1 September 2009, when the dependencies were raised to equal status with St.&#160;Helena and the territories changed its name from "Saint Helena and Dependencies" to "Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha".
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="World_War_II_and_subsequent_military_presence">World War II and subsequent military presence</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: World War II and subsequent military presence">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>During the <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_the_Atlantic" title="Battle of the Atlantic">Battle of the Atlantic</a> of <a href="/wiki/World_War_II" title="World War II">World War II</a> and the following several years of <a href="/wiki/U-boat" title="U-boat">U-boat</a> warfare in the Atlantic, both Saint&#160;Helena and Ascension Island were used by the <a href="/wiki/Allies_of_World_War_II" title="Allies of World War II">Allies</a> to base patrolling anti-surface-commerce-raider and <a href="/wiki/Anti-submarine_warfare" title="Anti-submarine warfare">anti-submarine warfare</a> (ASW) forces against the <a href="/wiki/Axis_powers" title="Axis powers">Axis powers</a>' naval units. Initially long range naval patrol <a href="/wiki/Flying_boat" title="Flying boat">flying boats</a> were used in the effort, and later in the war during the struggle to improve air coverage over the <a href="/wiki/Sea_lines_of_communication" title="Sea lines of communication">commercially important sea lanes</a>, air strips were built to support land based aircraft which supplied, augmented and complemented the <a href="/wiki/PBY_Catalina" class="mw-redirect" title="PBY Catalina">PBY Catalina</a> patrol planes in the vitally important ASW mission.
</p><p>The United Kingdom and the United States still jointly operate the airfield (<a href="/wiki/RAF_Ascension_Island" title="RAF Ascension Island">RAF Ascension Island</a>) on Ascension, which also serves as a space-based communications, <a href="/wiki/Signals_intelligence" title="Signals intelligence">signals intelligence</a>, and navigation nexus and hub (Ground station). One of only four <a href="/wiki/GPS" class="mw-redirect" title="GPS">GPS</a> satellite ground antennas is located there.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Geography">Geography</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Geography">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-left" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Edinburgh-Tristan.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/fb/Edinburgh-Tristan.jpg/220px-Edinburgh-Tristan.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="165" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/fb/Edinburgh-Tristan.jpg/330px-Edinburgh-Tristan.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/fb/Edinburgh-Tristan.jpg/440px-Edinburgh-Tristan.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2386" data-file-height="1790" /></a><figcaption><a href="/wiki/Edinburgh_of_the_Seven_Seas" title="Edinburgh of the Seven Seas">Edinburgh of the Seven Seas</a>, Tristan da Cunha</figcaption></figure>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/Category:Geography_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Category:Geography of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha">Category:Geography of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</a> and <a href="/wiki/List_of_mountains_and_hills_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="List of mountains and hills of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha">List of mountains and hills of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</a></div>
<p>The territories stretch across a huge distance of the South Atlantic Ocean with the northernmost island, Ascension, having a <a href="/wiki/Latitude" title="Latitude">latitude</a> of <span class="nowrap">7° 56′ S</span> of the equator and the southernmost island, <a href="/wiki/Gough_Island" title="Gough Island">Gough Island</a>, at <span class="nowrap">40° 19′ S</span>. Between Saint Helena and Tristan da Cunha is the <a href="/wiki/Tropic_of_Capricorn" title="Tropic of Capricorn">Tropic of Capricorn</a>. The distance between the northern tip of Ascension Island and the southern tip of Gough Island is 2,263 miles (3,642&#160;km) (an equivalent distance between London and the <a href="/wiki/Dead_Sea" title="Dead Sea">Dead Sea</a>). The three territories lie in the <a href="/wiki/Western_Hemisphere" title="Western Hemisphere">Western Hemisphere</a> and have the same time zone: <a href="/wiki/Greenwich_Mean_Time" title="Greenwich Mean Time">Greenwich Mean Time</a>. <a href="/wiki/Daylight_saving_time" title="Daylight saving time">Daylight saving time</a> is not observed.
</p><p>Although all three territories were formed by <a href="/wiki/Volcano" title="Volcano">volcanic</a> activity, only the Tristan da Cunha group of islands are volcanically active at the moment.
</p><p>The highest point of the territories is <a href="/wiki/Queen_Mary%27s_Peak" title="Queen Mary&#39;s Peak">Queen Mary's Peak</a> on the island of Tristan da Cunha, with an elevation of 2,062 metres (6,765&#160;ft) above sea level. The mountain is listed as an <a href="/wiki/Ultra_prominent_peak" class="mw-redirect" title="Ultra prominent peak">ultra prominent peak</a>.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Climate">Climate</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Climate">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Because of the massive distance from north to south (over 3,600&#160;km (2,200&#160;mi)), the territories have various climates. Ascension has a warm, arid climate, with temperatures all year long reaching above 20&#160;°C (68&#160;°F). St Helena is more moderate (and arid near the coasts). Tristan da Cunha, being closer to the <a href="/wiki/Antarctic_Circle" title="Antarctic Circle">Antarctic Circle</a>, is much cooler and a lot wetter. The uninhabited southernmost <a href="/wiki/Gough_Island" title="Gough Island">Gough Island</a> is wetter and has freezing winter temperatures.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Territorial_waters">Territorial waters</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Territorial waters">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Tristanfromspace.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/37/Tristanfromspace.jpg/330px-Tristanfromspace.jpg" decoding="async" width="330" height="220" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/37/Tristanfromspace.jpg/495px-Tristanfromspace.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/37/Tristanfromspace.jpg/660px-Tristanfromspace.jpg 2x" data-file-width="1440" data-file-height="960" /></a><figcaption>Tristan da Cunha on 6 February 2013, as seen from the <a href="/wiki/International_Space_Station" title="International Space Station">International Space Station</a></figcaption></figure>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Territorial_waters" title="Territorial waters">territorial waters</a> of the islands extend out to 12 nautical miles (22&#160;km) from their <a href="/wiki/Baseline_(sea)" title="Baseline (sea)">coastal baselines</a>. The <a href="/wiki/Exclusive_Economic_Zone" class="mw-redirect" title="Exclusive Economic Zone">Exclusive Economic Zones</a> (EEZs) extend 200 nautical miles (370&#160;km) from the islands' baselines. Despite their size, the three EEZs do not overlap or touch one another, nor do they reach the EEZs of any other country or territory. The territories have the <a href="/wiki/Exclusive_Economic_Zone#United_Kingdom" class="mw-redirect" title="Exclusive Economic Zone">largest EEZ</a> of any of the British overseas territories (indeed larger than the United Kingdom's) and if included in the <a href="/wiki/Exclusive_Economic_Zone#Rankings_by_area" class="mw-redirect" title="Exclusive Economic Zone">ranking of countries by size of EEZ</a>, the territories would be 21st, behind Portugal and ahead of the Philippines. Neither the islands nor their EEZs are the subject of any current international dispute.
</p>

<p>An application was made in 2008 by the United Kingdom to the <a href="/wiki/United_Nations_Commission_on_the_Limits_of_the_Continental_Shelf" class="mw-redirect" title="United Nations Commission on the Limits of the Continental Shelf">United Nations Commission on the Limits of the Continental Shelf</a> to extend the limit of the <a href="/wiki/Continental_shelf" title="Continental shelf">continental shelf</a> claim of Ascension Island beyond 200 nautical miles (370&#160;km). The Commission recommended in 2010 that the limit not be extended beyond the standard limit, based on scientific surveys.<sup id="cite_ref-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-9">&#91;9&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Administrative_divisions">Administrative divisions</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Administrative divisions">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>

<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/Politics_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Politics of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha">Politics of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</a></div>
<div style="clear:both;"></div><figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-right" typeof="mw:File/Frameless"><a href="/wiki/File:PAT_-_Saint_Helena.gif" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/bf/PAT_-_Saint_Helena.gif/400px-PAT_-_Saint_Helena.gif" decoding="async" width="400" height="436" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/bf/PAT_-_Saint_Helena.gif/600px-PAT_-_Saint_Helena.gif 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/bf/PAT_-_Saint_Helena.gif/800px-PAT_-_Saint_Helena.gif 2x" data-file-width="895" data-file-height="975" /></a><figcaption></figcaption></figure>
<p>Administratively, each territory of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha is governed by a council. The Governor of the territory presides over the <a href="/wiki/Legislative_Council_of_Saint_Helena" title="Legislative Council of Saint Helena">Saint Helena Legislative Council</a>, and an Administrator on Ascension Island and an Administrator on Tristan da Cunha preside over these two areas' Island Councils. See <a href="#Constitution">Constitution section</a> below.
</p>

<p>The island of St Helena is then further divided into <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena#Administrative_divisions" title="Saint Helena">eight districts</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-10">&#91;10&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Constitution">Constitution</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Constitution">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>

</div></td></tr><tr><td class="sidebar-navbar"><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1129693374"><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1063604349">.mw-parser-output .navbar{display:inline;font-size:88%;font-weight:normal}.mw-parser-output .navbar-collapse{float:left;text-align:left}.mw-parser-output .navbar-boxtext{word-spacing:0}.mw-parser-output .navbar ul{display:inline-block;white-space:nowrap;line-height:inherit}.mw-parser-output .navbar-brackets::before{margin-right:-0.125em;content:"[ "}.mw-parser-output .navbar-brackets::after{margin-left:-0.125em;content:" ]"}.mw-parser-output .navbar li{word-spacing:-0.125em}.mw-parser-output .navbar a>span,.mw-parser-output .navbar a>abbr{text-decoration:inherit}.mw-parser-output .navbar-mini abbr{font-variant:small-caps;border-bottom:none;text-decoration:none;cursor:inherit}.mw-parser-output .navbar-ct-full{font-size:114%;margin:0 7em}.mw-parser-output .navbar-ct-mini{font-size:114%;margin:0 4em}</style><div class="navbar plainlinks hlist navbar-mini"><ul><li class="nv-view"><a href="/wiki/Template:Politics_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Template:Politics of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha"><abbr title="View this template">v</abbr></a></li><li class="nv-talk"><a href="/w/index.php?title=Template_talk:Politics_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Template talk:Politics of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha (page does not exist)"><abbr title="Discuss this template">t</abbr></a></li><li class="nv-edit"><a class="external text" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Template:Politics_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit"><abbr title="Edit this template">e</abbr></a></li></ul></div></td></tr></tbody></table>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/The_St_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha_Constitution_Order_2009" title="The St Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha Constitution Order 2009">The St Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha Constitution Order 2009</a></div>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:2019_Reuni%C3%A3o_Bilateral_com_o_Pr%C3%ADncipe_Charles_-_48948389972_(cropped).jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e2/2019_Reuni%C3%A3o_Bilateral_com_o_Pr%C3%ADncipe_Charles_-_48948389972_%28cropped%29.jpg/170px-2019_Reuni%C3%A3o_Bilateral_com_o_Pr%C3%ADncipe_Charles_-_48948389972_%28cropped%29.jpg" decoding="async" width="170" height="233" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e2/2019_Reuni%C3%A3o_Bilateral_com_o_Pr%C3%ADncipe_Charles_-_48948389972_%28cropped%29.jpg/255px-2019_Reuni%C3%A3o_Bilateral_com_o_Pr%C3%ADncipe_Charles_-_48948389972_%28cropped%29.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/e/e2/2019_Reuni%C3%A3o_Bilateral_com_o_Pr%C3%ADncipe_Charles_-_48948389972_%28cropped%29.jpg/340px-2019_Reuni%C3%A3o_Bilateral_com_o_Pr%C3%ADncipe_Charles_-_48948389972_%28cropped%29.jpg 2x" data-file-width="1073" data-file-height="1471" /></a><figcaption>Charles III is the head of state of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Government_House_Ascension_Island.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9f/Government_House_Ascension_Island.jpg/220px-Government_House_Ascension_Island.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="165" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9f/Government_House_Ascension_Island.jpg/330px-Government_House_Ascension_Island.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9f/Government_House_Ascension_Island.jpg/440px-Government_House_Ascension_Island.jpg 2x" data-file-width="1024" data-file-height="768" /></a><figcaption>Ascension Government House</figcaption></figure>
<p>The <i>St Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha Constitution Order 2009</i> (an <a href="/wiki/Order_in_Council" title="Order in Council">Order in Council</a> of the <a href="/wiki/Privy_Council_of_the_United_Kingdom" class="mw-redirect" title="Privy Council of the United Kingdom">Privy Council of the United Kingdom</a>) enacted a new constitution for the territory, which came into effect on 1 September 2009, and elevated Ascension Island and Tristan da Cunha from being dependencies of Saint Helena to equal constituent parts. Each constituent part has its own government, however the constitution order states that <a href="/wiki/Governor_of_Saint_Helena" title="Governor of Saint Helena">Governor of Saint Helena</a> is <i><a href="/wiki/Ex_officio" class="mw-redirect" title="Ex officio">ex officio</a></i> also the <a href="/wiki/Governor_of_Ascension" title="Governor of Ascension">Governor of Ascension</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Governor_of_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Governor of Tristan da Cunha">Governor of Tristan da Cunha</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-11">&#91;11&#93;</a></sup> Due to the distance between the three constituent parts of the territory the Ascension and Tristan da Cunha each have an Administrator who represents the Governor when they are off island. Notably the constitution includes (for each territory) the "<a href="/wiki/Human_rights" title="Human rights">fundamental rights and freedoms of individuals</a>".<sup id="cite_ref-constitution_12-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-constitution-12">&#91;12&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<table class="wikitable">

<tbody><tr>

<p>Saint Helena also has an Executive Council. The Governor of Saint Helena is the <a href="/wiki/British_monarch" class="mw-redirect" title="British monarch">British monarch</a>'s representative. The three territories share the same Attorney General, and the same <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena_Supreme_Court" class="mw-redirect" title="Saint Helena Supreme Court">Supreme Court</a> and Court of Appeal.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Police_and_defence">Police and defence</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Police and defence">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Royal_Saint_Helena_Police_Service" title="Royal Saint Helena Police Service">Royal Saint Helena Police Service</a> is responsible for policing on the islands.<sup id="cite_ref-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-13">&#91;13&#93;</a></sup> Defence is the responsibility of the United Kingdom, though no military forces are stationed on either Saint Helena or Tristan da Cunha. The <a href="/wiki/RAF_Ascension_Island" title="RAF Ascension Island">Royal Air Force maintains a staging base</a> at Ascension Island as part of <a href="/wiki/Military_of_the_Falkland_Islands" title="Military of the Falkland Islands">British military forces in the South Atlantic</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-14">&#91;14&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Education">Education</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Education">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Education_in_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Education in Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha">Education in Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</a></div>
<p>Saint Helena has multiple schools, including <a href="/wiki/Prince_Andrew_School_(Saint_Helena)" title="Prince Andrew School (Saint Helena)">Prince Andrew School</a>. Ascension has <a href="/wiki/Two_Boats_School" title="Two Boats School">Two Boats School</a>. Tristan da Cunha also has its own school.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Religion">Religion</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Religion">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1034237262">.mw-parser-output .stack{box-sizing:border-box}.mw-parser-output .stack>div{margin:1px;overflow:hidden}@media all and (min-width:720px){.mw-parser-output .stack-clear-left{float:left;clear:left}.mw-parser-output .stack-clear-right{float:right;clear:right}.mw-parser-output .stack-left{float:left}.mw-parser-output .stack-right{float:right}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-clear-left{float:left;clear:left;margin-right:1em}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-clear-right{float:right;clear:right;margin-left:1em}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-left{float:left;margin-right:1em}.mw-parser-output .stack-margin-right{float:right;margin-left:1em}}</style><div class="stack mw-stack stack-right"><div><figure class="mw-default-size" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:ASCENSION_ISLAND_-_ST._MARY%27S_ANGLICAN_CHURCH.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/14/ASCENSION_ISLAND_-_ST._MARY%27S_ANGLICAN_CHURCH.jpg/220px-ASCENSION_ISLAND_-_ST._MARY%27S_ANGLICAN_CHURCH.jpg" decoding="async" width="220" height="143" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/14/ASCENSION_ISLAND_-_ST._MARY%27S_ANGLICAN_CHURCH.jpg/330px-ASCENSION_ISLAND_-_ST._MARY%27S_ANGLICAN_CHURCH.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/14/ASCENSION_ISLAND_-_ST._MARY%27S_ANGLICAN_CHURCH.jpg/440px-ASCENSION_ISLAND_-_ST._MARY%27S_ANGLICAN_CHURCH.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2877" data-file-height="1874" /></a><figcaption><a href="/w/index.php?title=St._Mary%27s_Anglican_Church&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="St. Mary&#39;s Anglican Church (page does not exist)">St. Mary's Anglican Church</a><span class="noprint" style="font-size:85%; font-style: normal;">&#160;&#91;<a href="https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/St._Mary%E2%80%99s_Church_(Ascension)" class="extiw" title="de:St. Mary’s Church (Ascension)">de</a>&#93;</span>, Ascension</figcaption></figure></div></div>
<p>Most residents of St. Helena belong to the <a href="/wiki/Anglican_Communion" title="Anglican Communion">Anglican Communion</a> through the <a href="/wiki/Anglican_Church_of_Southern_Africa" title="Anglican Church of Southern Africa">Anglican Church of Southern Africa</a> and are members of the <a href="/wiki/Diocese_of_St_Helena" title="Diocese of St Helena">Diocese of St Helena</a>, which has its own bishop and includes Ascension Island.
</p><p>Roman Catholics are pastorally served by the <a href="/wiki/Missio_sui_iuris_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" class="mw-redirect" title="Missio sui iuris of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha">Missio sui iuris of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</a>, whose office of ecclesiastical superior is vested in the <a href="/wiki/Apostolic_Prefecture_of_the_Falkland_Islands" title="Apostolic Prefecture of the Falkland Islands">Apostolic Prefecture of the Falkland Islands</a>.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Currency">Currency</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: Currency">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena_pound" title="Saint Helena pound">Saint Helena pound</a></div>
<p>In 1821 a copper <a href="/wiki/Halfpenny_(British_pre-decimal_coin)" title="Halfpenny (British pre-decimal coin)">halfpenny</a> was struck specifically for use in St. Helena, which subsequently intermingled with British coinage.
</p><p>Saint Helena used <a href="/wiki/Pound_sterling" title="Pound sterling">sterling currency</a> as in the United Kingdom until 1976 when it began to issue its own banknotes at par with sterling. In 1984, the territory also began to issue its own coinage for both St. Helena and Ascension Island, with the same sizes as the coinage of the United Kingdom. Also similar to British coinage, Queen Elizabeth II is found on the obverse but the reverse have quite different designs referring to the territory. Whereas the coins are struck with "Saint Helena • Ascension", the banknotes only say "Government of St. Helena". Commemorative coins are struck separately for the two entities, and say either just "St. Helena" or "Ascencion Island". The Saint Helena pound also circulates on Ascension Island, but not in the other territory, <a href="/wiki/Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Tristan da Cunha">Tristan da Cunha</a>, where UK currency circulates.
</p><p>The Currency Commissioners, part of the <a href="/wiki/Politics_of_Saint_Helena" title="Politics of Saint Helena">Government of Saint Helena</a>, issue the <a href="/wiki/Coins_of_the_Saint_Helena_pound" class="mw-redirect" title="Coins of the Saint Helena pound">St Helena pound banknotes and coins</a>. There is no <a href="/wiki/Central_bank" title="Central bank">central bank</a>; the currency is pegged to the pound sterling which is controlled by the <a href="/wiki/Bank_of_England" title="Bank of England">Bank of England</a> in London. The <a href="/wiki/Bank_of_Saint_Helena" class="mw-redirect" title="Bank of Saint Helena">Bank of Saint Helena</a> is the territory's only bank. The bank sets its own deposit and lending rates and has branches in Jamestown on Saint Helena and Georgetown on Ascension Island. Although the bank does not have a physical presence on Tristan da Cunha, the residents of Tristan are entitled to use its services.<sup id="cite_ref-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-15">&#91;15&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Communications">Communications</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Communications">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Communications_in_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Communications in Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha">Communications in Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</a></div>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Telecommunications">Telecommunications</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Telecommunications">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><a href="/w/index.php?title=Sure_South_Atlantic&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="Sure South Atlantic (page does not exist)">Sure South Atlantic</a> provide the telecommunications service in the territories. Saint Helena has the <a href="/wiki/List_of_country_calling_codes" title="List of country calling codes">international calling code</a> <a href="/wiki/%2B290" class="mw-redirect" title="+290">+290</a> which, since 2006, Tristan da Cunha shares. Since 2013, telephone numbers have been five digits long, with fixed line numbers beginning with "2" and mobile numbers with "5" or "6".<sup id="cite_ref-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-16">&#91;16&#93;</a></sup> Ascension Island has a separate country code, <a href="/wiki/%2B247" class="mw-redirect" title="+247">+247</a> and also has five-digit numbers on the island, having changed in 2015.<sup id="cite_ref-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-17">&#91;17&#93;</a></sup> Ascension Island also held an extensive broadcast facility for international shortwave transmissions to Africa and South America.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Mail">Mail</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Mail">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Ascension Island, Tristan da Cunha and Saint Helena all issue their own postage stamps, which provide a significant income. The three territories each have their own <a href="/wiki/Royal_Mail" title="Royal Mail">Royal Mail</a> <a href="/wiki/Postal_code" title="Postal code">postal code</a>: 
</p>
<ul><li>Ascension Island: ASCN 1ZZ</li>
<li>Saint Helena: STHL 1ZZ</li>
<li>Tristan da Cunha: TDCU 1ZZ</li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Flags">Flags</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Flags">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>The <a href="/wiki/Flag_of_the_United_Kingdom" title="Flag of the United Kingdom">Flag of the United Kingdom</a> is used for all official purposes; and each of the three territories has its own flag for official use. Between 2002 and 2013 Saint Helena and Tristan da Cunha each had their own separate flags, whilst Ascension Island used the <a href="/wiki/Union_Flag" class="mw-redirect" title="Union Flag">Union Flag</a>, and before 2002 the flag of Saint Helena was used in Tristan da Cunha for all official purposes.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Saint_Helena">Saint Helena</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=18" title="Edit section: Saint Helena">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/Flag_of_Saint_Helena" title="Flag of Saint Helena">Flag of Saint Helena</a></div>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Flag_of_Saint_Helena.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/00/Flag_of_Saint_Helena.svg/250px-Flag_of_Saint_Helena.svg.png" decoding="async" width="250" height="125" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/00/Flag_of_Saint_Helena.svg/375px-Flag_of_Saint_Helena.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/00/Flag_of_Saint_Helena.svg/500px-Flag_of_Saint_Helena.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1200" data-file-height="600" /></a><figcaption>The flag of Saint Helena</figcaption></figure>
<p>The flag of <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena" title="Saint Helena">Saint Helena</a> was adopted on 4 October 1984. It is a <a href="/wiki/Defacement_(flag)" title="Defacement (flag)">defaced</a> (i.e. differentiated) <a href="/wiki/Blue_Ensign" title="Blue Ensign">Blue Ensign</a>, i.e. a blue field with the <a href="/wiki/Union_Jack" title="Union Jack">Union Jack</a> in the upper hoist-side quadrant and the shield from the <a href="/wiki/Coat_of_arms_of_Saint_Helena" title="Coat of arms of Saint Helena">coat of arms of Saint Helena</a> centred on the outer half of the flag. The shield features a rocky coastline and a three-masted sailing ship, with a <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena_plover" title="Saint Helena plover">Saint Helena plover</a>, also known as a wirebird, atop. It was updated in 2018 to depict a more realistic looking wirebird.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Ascension_Island">Ascension Island</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Ascension Island">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/Flag_of_Ascension_Island" title="Flag of Ascension Island">Flag of Ascension Island</a></div>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Flag_of_Ascension_Island.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/65/Flag_of_Ascension_Island.svg/250px-Flag_of_Ascension_Island.svg.png" decoding="async" width="250" height="125" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/65/Flag_of_Ascension_Island.svg/375px-Flag_of_Ascension_Island.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/65/Flag_of_Ascension_Island.svg/500px-Flag_of_Ascension_Island.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1000" data-file-height="500" /></a><figcaption>The flag of Ascension Island</figcaption></figure>
<p>The flag of <a href="/wiki/Ascension_Island" title="Ascension Island">Ascension Island</a> was adopted on 11 May 2013.<sup id="cite_ref-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-18">&#91;18&#93;</a></sup> The flag is a <a href="/wiki/Blue_ensign" class="mw-redirect" title="Blue ensign">blue ensign</a> design, defaced with the <a href="/wiki/Coat_of_arms_of_Ascension_Island" title="Coat of arms of Ascension Island">coat of arms of Ascension Island</a>. Prior to the adoption of this flag, the island used the <a href="/wiki/Union_Flag" class="mw-redirect" title="Union Flag">Union Flag</a> of the United Kingdom for official purposes.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Tristan_da_Cunha">Tristan da Cunha</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=20" title="Edit section: Tristan da Cunha">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Flag of Tristan da Cunha">Flag of Tristan da Cunha</a></div>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/89/Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha.svg/250px-Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha.svg.png" decoding="async" width="250" height="125" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/89/Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha.svg/375px-Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/8/89/Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha.svg/500px-Flag_of_Tristan_da_Cunha.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="1000" data-file-height="500" /></a><figcaption>The flag of Tristan da Cunha</figcaption></figure>
<p>The flag of <a href="/wiki/Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Tristan da Cunha">Tristan da Cunha</a> was adopted on 20 October 2002, in a proclamation made by the <a href="/wiki/Governor_of_Saint_Helena" title="Governor of Saint Helena">Governor of Saint Helena</a> under a <a href="/wiki/Royal_warrant_(document)" class="mw-redirect" title="Royal warrant (document)">Royal Warrant</a> granted by <a href="/wiki/Elizabeth_II" title="Elizabeth II">Queen Elizabeth II</a>. Prior to this, as a dependency of <a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena" title="Saint Helena">Saint Helena</a>, Tristan da Cunha used the <a href="/wiki/Flag_of_Saint_Helena" title="Flag of Saint Helena">flag of Saint Helena</a> for official purposes.
</p><p>The flag is a <a href="/wiki/Blue_ensign" class="mw-redirect" title="Blue ensign">blue ensign</a> design, defaced with the <a href="/wiki/Coat_of_arms_of_Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Coat of arms of Tristan da Cunha">coat of arms of Tristan da Cunha</a> – a Tristan longboat above a Naval Crown, with a central shield decorated with four yellow-nosed <a href="/wiki/Albatross" title="Albatross">albatrosses</a> and flanked by two <a href="/wiki/Tristan_rock_lobster" class="mw-redirect" title="Tristan rock lobster">Tristan rock lobsters</a>. Below this is a scroll with the territory's motto, <i>Our faith is our strength</i>.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Transport">Transport</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=21" title="Edit section: Transport">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1033289096"><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">See also: <a href="/wiki/Transport_on_Saint_Helena" title="Transport on Saint Helena">Transport on Saint Helena</a></div>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Maritime_transport">Maritime transport</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=22" title="Edit section: Maritime transport">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Each of the three main islands has a harbour or small port, situated in the islands' chief settlement (Georgetown, Jamestown, and Edinburgh). In addition St. Helena has a 118 m long permanent wharf facility, built as part of the airport project, in Rupert's Bay for bulk, containerised and general cargos as well as passenger landings.<sup id="cite_ref-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-19">&#91;19&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Airports">Airports</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=23" title="Edit section: Airports">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:First_Comair_Boeing_737-800_flight_to_Saint_Helena_Airport_(191).jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/ba/First_Comair_Boeing_737-800_flight_to_Saint_Helena_Airport_%28191%29.jpg/250px-First_Comair_Boeing_737-800_flight_to_Saint_Helena_Airport_%28191%29.jpg" decoding="async" width="250" height="102" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/ba/First_Comair_Boeing_737-800_flight_to_Saint_Helena_Airport_%28191%29.jpg/375px-First_Comair_Boeing_737-800_flight_to_Saint_Helena_Airport_%28191%29.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/ba/First_Comair_Boeing_737-800_flight_to_Saint_Helena_Airport_%28191%29.jpg/500px-First_Comair_Boeing_737-800_flight_to_Saint_Helena_Airport_%28191%29.jpg 2x" data-file-width="2048" data-file-height="833" /></a><figcaption>The airport of Saint Helena</figcaption></figure>
<p><a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena_Airport" title="Saint Helena Airport">St. Helena Airport</a> received its first scheduled commercial flight on 14 October 2017.<sup id="cite_ref-20" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-20">&#91;20&#93;</a></sup> Commercial flights, operated by Airlink using an <a href="/wiki/Embraer_E-Jet_family" title="Embraer E-Jet family">Embraer E190</a>, are scheduled from <a href="/wiki/O._R._Tambo_International_Airport" title="O. R. Tambo International Airport">OR Tambo Airport</a> in Johannesburg each Saturday, returning the same day (or Sunday when the extension to Ascension Island is operating).
</p><p>Commercial flights between St. Helena and Ascension Island operate on the second Saturday of each month, with the aircraft returning to St. Helena on the Sunday, before continuing on to Johannesburg.<sup id="cite_ref-21" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-21">&#91;21&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>There is a military airfield on Ascension Island (<a href="/wiki/RAF_Ascension_Island" title="RAF Ascension Island">RAF Ascension Island</a>), though potholes on the runway resulted in the April 2017 cancellation of all but essential personnel/supply flights as well as emergency medical evacuations.<sup id="cite_ref-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-22">&#91;22&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-23" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-23">&#91;23&#93;</a></sup> Regular <a href="/wiki/Royal_Air_Force" title="Royal Air Force">RAF</a> flights connected Ascension with <a href="/wiki/RAF_Brize_Norton" title="RAF Brize Norton">RAF Brize Norton</a> in the UK and <a href="/wiki/RAF_Mount_Pleasant" title="RAF Mount Pleasant">RAF Mount Pleasant</a> in the <a href="/wiki/Falkland_Islands" title="Falkland Islands">Falkland Islands</a>, a transport link called the South Atlantic Air Bridge. The flights are mainly to transport military personnel, though the RAF did allow fare-paying civilians to use them. Ascension Island is also used by the US military (which supply the base using <a href="/wiki/MV_Ascension" title="MV Ascension">MV <i>Ascension</i></a>) and was a designated emergency landing site for the <a href="/wiki/Space_Shuttle_program" title="Space Shuttle program">Space Shuttle program</a>.
</p><p>The islands of Tristan da Cunha can only be accessed by sea due to the lack of an airport.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Vehicular_traffic">Vehicular traffic</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=24" title="Edit section: Vehicular traffic">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p><a href="/wiki/Saint_Helena" title="Saint Helena">Saint Helena</a> has 138 kilometres (86&#160;mi)—118 kilometres (73&#160;mi) paved and 20 kilometres (12&#160;mi) unpaved—of roads. <a href="/wiki/Tristan_da_Cunha" title="Tristan da Cunha">Tristan da Cunha</a> has approximately 10 kilometres (6&#160;mi) of paved roads, while <a href="/wiki/Ascension_Island" title="Ascension Island">Ascension</a> has around 40 kilometres (25&#160;mi) paved.<sup id="cite_ref-24" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-24">&#91;24&#93;</a></sup> Each island has its <a href="/wiki/Vehicle_registration_plates_of_Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha" class="mw-redirect" title="Vehicle registration plates of Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha">own vehicle registration plate system</a>. Traffic <a href="/wiki/Right-_and_left-hand_traffic" class="mw-redirect" title="Right- and left-hand traffic">drives on the left</a> in all three territories, as in the United Kingdom. Two of the nearest countries to the islands—<a href="/wiki/South_Africa" title="South Africa">South Africa</a> and <a href="/wiki/Namibia" title="Namibia">Namibia</a>—also drive on the left.
</p>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Further_reading">Further reading</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=Saint_Helena,_Ascension_and_Tristan_da_Cunha&amp;action=edit&amp;section=27" title="Edit section: Further reading">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1054258005">.mw-parser-output .refbegin{font-size:90%;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .refbegin-hanging-indents>ul{margin-left:0}.mw-parser-output .refbegin-hanging-indents>ul>li{margin-left:0;padding-left:3.2em;text-indent:-3.2em}.mw-parser-output .refbegin-hanging-indents ul,.mw-parser-output .refbegin-hanging-indents ul li{list-style:none}@media(max-width:720px){.mw-parser-output .refbegin-hanging-indents>ul>li{padding-left:1.6em;text-indent:-1.6em}}.mw-parser-output .refbegin-columns{margin-top:0.3em}.mw-parser-output .refbegin-columns ul{margin-top:0}.mw-parser-output .refbegin-columns li{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid-column}</style><div class="refbegin refbegin-columns references-column-width" style="column-width: 40em">
<ul><li>Barrow, K. M. – <i>Three Years in Tristan da Cunha.</i></li>
<li>Booy, D. M. – <i>Rock of Exile: A Narrative of Tristan da Cunha.</i></li>
<li>Brander, J. – <i>Tristan da Cunha, 1506-1902.</i></li>
<li>Brinck, Per – <i>Coleoptera of Tristan da Cunha.</i></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Arnold_Chaplin" title="Arnold Chaplin">Chaplin, Arnold</a> – <i>Thomas Short (Principal Medical Officer of St. Helena). With Biographies of Some Other Medical Men Associated with the Case of Napoleon From 1815-1821.</i></li>
<li>Christopherson, Erling – <i>Tristan da Cunha, the Lonely Isle.</i></li>
<li>Christopherson, Erling and R. L. Benham – <i>Tristan da Cunha, the Lonely Isle.</i></li>
<li>Crawford, Allan – <i>Tristan da Cunha and the 'Roaring Forties'.</i></li>
<li>Gane, Douglas M. – <i>Tristan da Cunha: An Empire Outpost and Its Keepers.</i> (<a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://penelope.uchicago.edu/Thayer/E/Gazetteer/Places/Africa/Tristan_da_Cunha/_Texts/GANTDC/home.html">complete transcription online</a>)</li>
<li>Gill, Mrs. – <i>Six Months in Ascension 1, Vol., 12 mo</i></li>
<li>Gosse, Philip – <i>Helena, 1502-1938.</i></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Basil_Hall" title="Basil Hall">Hall, Basil</a>, Captain – <i>Voyage to the Eastern Seas in the Year 1816; Including an Account of Captain Maxwell’s Attack on the Batteries at Canton; And Notes of an Interview with Bonaparte at St. Helena, in August 1817.</i></li>
<li>Kemble, James – <i>St. Helena During Napoleon’s Exile: Gorrequer’s Diary.</i></li>
<li>Kiser, C. V. – <i>A Study of St.-Helena Islanders in Harlem and Other Urban Centers.</i></li>
<li>Mackay, Margaret – <i>Angry Island: The Story of Tristan da Cunha, 1506-1963.</i>  (<a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="https://penelope.uchicago.edu/Thayer/E/Gazetteer/Places/Africa/Tristan_da_Cunha/_Texts/MACAI/home.html">complete transcription online</a>)</li>
<li>Martineau, Gilbert and Frances Partridge – <i>Napoleon’s St. Helena.</i></li>
<li>Masson, Frederic and Louis B. Frewer – <i>Napoleon at St. Helena, 1815-1821.</i></li>
<li>Munch, Peter A. – <i>Crisis in Utopia: The Ordeal of Tristan da Cunha.</i></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Peter_A._Munch" title="Peter A. Munch">Munch, Peter A.</a> – <i>Sociology of Tristan da Cunha: Results of the Norwegian Scientific Expedition to Tristan da Cunha, 1937-8, No. 13.</i></li>
<li>Munch, Peter A. – <i>The Song Tradition of Tristan da Cunha.</i></li>
<li>Rowlands, Beau W., Trevor Trueman, Storrs L. Olson, M. Neil McCulloch, and Richard K. Brooke – <i>The Birds of St. Helena.</i></li>
<li>Shine, Ian and Reynold Gold – <i>Serendipity in St. Helena: A Genetical and Medical Study of an Isolated Community.</i></li>
<li>Stewart, C. S. – <i>A Visit to the South Seas in the <a href="/wiki/USS_Vincennes_(1826)" title="USS Vincennes (1826)">United States' Ship Vincennes</a>, during the Years 1829 and 1830, with Scenes in Brazil, Peru, Manila, the Cape of Good Hope, and St. Helena.</i></li>
<li>Stonehouse, Bernard – <i>Wideawake Island: The Story of the B. O. U. Centenary Expedition to Ascension.</i></li>
<li>Wace, N. M. and M. W. Holdgate – <i>The Vegetation of Tristan da Cunha.</i></li>
<li>Weider, Ben and Sten Forschufvud – <i>Assassination at St. Helena Revisited.</i></li>
<li>Zettersten, Arne – <i>The English of Tristan da Cunha.</i></li></ul>

</div></td></tr><tr><td class="navbox-abovebelow" colspan="2"><div>
<ul><li>23.&#160;Since 2009 part of <u><a class="mw-selflink selflink">Saint Helena, Ascension and Tristan da Cunha</a></u>; Ascension Island (1922–) and Tristan da Cunha (1938–) were previously dependencies of Saint Helena.</li>
<li>24.&#160;Both claimed in 1908; territories formed in 1962 (British Antarctic Territory) and 1985 (South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands).</li></ul>
</div></td></tr></tbody></table><div></div></td></tr><tr><td class="navbox-abovebelow hlist" colspan="2"><div>
